Gladiador: roteiro Tripo do piloto ao nível 150 (copia-e-cola)

Passada de personalidade 19/11 (régua nº 1 do criador: "hoje TUDO é visual", herói nível Lost Ark / Diablo IV desde o nível 1).
1. Identidade nova (§0): campeão invicto da Cova, hoplita heroico; crista carmim alta sempre na cabeça (cresce a cada série), capa carmim, braços nus, couraça esculpida, saiote de pteruges; detalhe-assinatura: fichas de vitória (discos de bronze lisos até o 26, medalhões phalerae do 31 em diante), mais a cada nível.
2. Contraste: cobre avermelhado + carmim forte (a cor mais viva) + marfim #E8DCC0 como terceiro tom claro; proibido o boneco todo marrom e o ouro amarelo dominante (é o Guardião).
3. Piloto: 1.1 gera um conceito por nó (o criador varia pela quantidade de imagens); 1.2 pose T; §2 mulher com corte feminino real (cláusula FEMALE CUT em toda MULHER).
4. Níveis 1-60 reescritos: BULK no 26, 37, 44 e 55; intermediários com ≥2 marcas de longe novas (15 linotórax marfim, 44 crista dupla e borda marfim / galerus alto e fitas, 55 cauda de crina marfim / cinto de fitas); nível 60 renomeado "Bronze de Triunfo" (o "escuro" saiu) com SHOWPIECE, ouro velho e peça-troféu nas costas.
5. 61-150: só o bloco de identidade foi trocado. Capa curta antes do 41 mantida como peça-assinatura (Leônidas), agora limitada à cintura; decisão aberta ao criador.
Status: pronto para colar, 19/11 (passada de personalidade aplicada). Nada foi gerado. Autor: Claude (art-director). Modelo: design/tripo/barbaro-cadeia-armaduras-1-150.md (mesmos níveis, chaves e intermediárias, modelo de ramos do 41 ao 150: ITEM neutro, HOMEM e MULHER por ramo, ARMA única).
Fontes: design/art/visual-equipamento-por-classe.md (§3, §4, §10, §11, §12), design/gdd/gladiador.md (§3.2, §3.4, §3.6, §3.7, §3.9; hoplita, aspis de 0,90 m, Cova de Pedrabrota), design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md §1.11, design/art/cores-classes-distancia-2026-11-19.md (cobre #A8552E, não #B5703A), design/narrative/historias-das-classes-v2.md §7. Entrada só de imagens nossas (nunca Asset Store nem Fab; a capa de livro de referência não se anexa).
Ramos: A = Hoplita (40) → Capitão da Falange (60) → Estratego de Bronze (80) → Rei do Desfiladeiro (100) → Falange Viva (130): a linha, escudo grande, lança longa, crista transversal, capa até o chão. B = Reciário (40) → Mestre da Arena (60) → Invicto (80) → Campeão Eterno (100) → Arena Viva (130): a arena, escudo menor, rede carmim com pesos sobre o ombro esquerdo, galerus alto no ombro direito, fitas de vitória, capa ao joelho.

0. Resumo (5 linhas)

  1. Identidade: campeão invicto da Cova, hoplita heroico (Armadura pesada): couraça esculpida, braços nus, grevas de bronze, saiote de pteruges que abre no quadril; silhueta em V sob a crista. Nunca cavaleiro, nunca bárbaro.
  2. Cor e contraste: cobre-bronze avermelhado #A8552E (45-55%, com bordas gastas mais claras), carmim #C8102E forte na crista, capa e faixa (a cor mais viva), marfim #E8DCC0 (linho, barras, borda grega) como terceiro tom claro, couro escuro #3B2418 só nas tiras. Nunca monocromático marrom; nunca ouro amarelo dominante.
  3. Silhueta-assinatura: disco e crista: aspis grande à esquerda, lança vertical à direita, crista carmim alta no topo e capa carmim atrás (curta até o 40, só até a cintura; longa do 41). Detalhe-assinatura: fichas de vitória (discos de bronze lisos na faixa até o 26; medalhões phalerae no arnês do 31 em diante), mais a cada nível.
  4. Peça de cabeça fixa: elmo coríntio erguido na testa, rosto sempre visível, com crista de crina carmim que cresce a cada série (A: transversal do 41; B: longitudinal listrada de marfim).
  5. Arma: lança (dory) e aspis redondo (escudo com prompt evolutivo); a partir do 41 misturam a falange (comprimento, leão) e a arena (farpa, rede). Só o nível 150 tem brilho sutil.

GLADIATOR IDENTITY (bloco fixo; vai dentro de TODO prompt de armadura, HOMEM e MULHER, antes de "OUTFIT:"):

`` GLADIATOR IDENTITY (never lose this): the unbeaten champion of the quarry arena, a heroic Greek hoplite, NOT a knight and NOT a barbarian. Unforgettable silhouette: a TALL CRIMSON HORSEHAIR CREST on a bronze Corinthian helm that is ALWAYS on the head, worn pushed up high with the face fully visible (the crest grows taller and wider at every series), a crimson cape at the back (short, never below the waist, until level 40; long from level 41), a sculpted chest (ivory linen and leather at low level, a muscled bronze cuirass later), BARE muscular arms with bronze arm rings, a flared skirt of leather strips (pteruges) and tall bronze greaves: a broad V-shaped hero under a crest. Signature detail: champion VICTORY TOKENS, round bronze discs won in the arena, hung from the sash at low level and set as medallions (phalerae) on a chest harness later, more of them at every level, never fewer. COLOR CONTRAST (must read from far away): warm reddish copper-bronze #A8552E with lighter worn edges, STRONG saturated crimson #C8102E on the crest, cape and sash as the most vivid color of the figure, clean IVORY linen #E8DCC0 as a light third tone (under-tunic, hems, borders), dark leather #3B2418 only for straps; copper, crimson and ivory clearly separated, never a monochrome brown figure. Forbidden: medieval knight look, full plate, plate skirt, closed visor or face mask, big pointed pauldrons, chainmail, fur pelt, bones, skulls, teeth or tusk trophies, tribal war paint, chains, sleeves, robe, feather plume, heraldic kite shield, yellow-gold dominant armor, all-brown or all-leather monochrome, bare-chested loincloth-only look. ``

Forbidden, em português: cavaleiro medieval, placa inteira, saia de placas, viseira ou máscara, ombreira pontuda grande, cota de malha, pelego, ossos, crânios, presas, pintura de guerra (tudo isso é do Bárbaro ou do Guardião), correntes (Carrasco), mangas, roupão, pluma de pena, escudo heráldico em gota, ouro amarelo dominante, boneco todo marrom ou todo couro, "espartano de tanga".

MULHER (vale em toda MULHER da cadeia): cláusula FEMALE CUT (couraça moldada ao busto e ao abdômen, cintura marcada, pteruges abrindo no quadril, pernas longas, trança saindo do elmo; nunca "o homem afinado"); nos níveis com BULK, BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES, NOT IN HER BODY; termina sempre com Keep her feminine cut.

MÉTODO (decisão do criador): todo nível, chave ou intermediário, parte do CORPO BASE em pose T (§1.2 homem, §2 mulher). O prompt diz "IGNORE his/her current clothes and dress him/her in a COMPLETELY NEW level N outfit" e descreve a roupa inteira do zero. A continuidade vem da paleta e dos temas. LANÇA e ESCUDO partem do item do nível anterior, sempre REDESIGNED (cabeça e cabo da lança; face e borda/alça do escudo) e mais robustos. Na pose T as mãos ficam vazias: sem lança e sem escudo.

Modelo de ramos (do 41 ao 150): por nível há ITEM (a armadura neutra, sozinha, fundo carvão), HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A e MULHER-B e uma ARMA única (LANÇA e ESCUDO únicos por nível, misturando falange e arena). Níveis 1 a 37: HOMEM, MULHER, LANÇA e ESCUDO completos. No fim: arte de apresentação (níveis 50 e 150) e os 2 prompts fixos de item.

Regras da cadeia:

  • Todo nível troca peças E materiais: couraça, saiote, grevas/botas e braçadeiras sempre com desenho e material novos; nunca mais leve que o anterior (BULK nos intermediários 26, 37, 44, 55); intermediário com ≥2 marcas de longe novas em relação ao chave.
  • Troféus crescem e não regridem: fichas de vitória 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 (lisas), phalerae 7 → 9 → 11 → 13 → 15 → arnês cheio; crista sempre maior; anéis de braço acumulam.
  • Curva de poder: S1 = linho marfim e couro; S2 e S3 sóbrios (couro fervido, linotórax e bronze liso, sem gravura); ornamento (leão, borda grega, phalerae gravadas) só da S4 em diante; capa curta até a cintura antes do 41, capa LONGA só do 41 em diante; nunca crânios, pelego nem pintura de guerra.
  • Nível de forma (60, 80, 100, 130, 150) = "TCHA": cláusula SHOWPIECE + CHAMPION ACCENTS (ouro velho fosco, carmim forte, marfim) + peça-troféu nas costas. Escuro demais reprova.
  • Frente e costas com as mesmas peças; traje fosco, sem brilho (só o 150: brilho muito sutil). CRYSTAL TOUCH do 41 em diante.

1. Conceito do piloto masculino S1

1.1 Um conceito livre (nó "Gerar imagem"; texto no singular, um conceito por imagem; o criador varia pela quantidade de imagens do nó e escolhe 1):

`` Concept of ONE stylized fantasy RPG male Gladiator hero at level 1, a young champion of the quarry arena who has just won his first fight, full body, front view, standing tall and confident, heroic proportions: broad shoulders, V-shaped muscular torso, strong legs, plain grey background, neutral light. GLADIATOR IDENTITY (never lose this): the unbeaten champion of the quarry arena, a heroic Greek hoplite, NOT a knight and NOT a barbarian. Unforgettable silhouette: a TALL CRIMSON HORSEHAIR CREST on a bronze Corinthian helm that is ALWAYS on the head, worn pushed up high with the face fully visible (the crest grows taller and wider at every series), a crimson cape at the back (short, never below the waist, until level 40; long from level 41), a sculpted chest (ivory linen and leather at low level, a muscled bronze cuirass later), BARE muscular arms with bronze arm rings, a flared skirt of leather strips (pteruges) and tall bronze greaves: a broad V-shaped hero under a crest. Signature detail: champion VICTORY TOKENS, round bronze discs won in the arena, hung from the sash at low level and set as medallions (phalerae) on a chest harness later, more of them at every level, never fewer. COLOR CONTRAST (must read from far away): warm reddish copper-bronze #A8552E with lighter worn edges, STRONG saturated crimson #C8102E on the crest, cape and sash as the most vivid color of the figure, clean IVORY linen #E8DCC0 as a light third tone (under-tunic, hems, borders), dark leather #3B2418 only for straps; copper, crimson and ivory clearly separated, never a monochrome brown figure. Forbidden: medieval knight look, full plate, plate skirt, closed visor or face mask, big pointed pauldrons, chainmail, fur pelt, bones, skulls, teeth or tusk trophies, tribal war paint, chains, sleeves, robe, feather plume, heraldic kite shield, yellow-gold dominant armor, all-brown or all-leather monochrome, bare-chested loincloth-only look. Very simple level 1 OUTFIT, simple but unforgettable: a plain undyed IVORY linen tunic to mid-thigh with a crimson hem stripe, a single leather chest strap, a wide crimson sash over a rope belt with ONE small plain bronze victory token hanging from it, bare muscular arms with leather wrist straps and a thin bronze arm ring, strapped sandals laced to mid-calf, a plain bronze Corinthian helm pushed up on the forehead with a short stiff crimson horsehair crest brush, face fully visible. A short plain crimson linen cape to the shoulder blades tied at the throat. He holds a plain round wooden aspis painted copper-red with a bronze rim on the left arm and a simple one-handed spear with a leaf-shaped bronze head in the right hand. No cuirass, no ornament. AAA action-RPG hero quality, hand-painted stylized look, matte saturated colors, crimson, copper and ivory clearly separated, readable silhouette from far away, no glow, no text. ``

1.2 Pose T do escolhido (entrada = o conceito escolhido; é o CORPO BASE masculino de toda a cadeia):

`` Same Gladiator as the input image, same face and body, same level 1 outfit, same crimson crest and cape, symmetric T-pose, arms open, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, no shield, front view, full body, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no glow. ``

2. Corpo feminino base

Entrada: o piloto masculino em pose T. Esta imagem é o CORPO BASE feminino.

`` Same Gladiator outfit as the input image, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. Make the character a young HEROINE with a real female body, NOT a slimmed-down man: athletic feminine build, defined bust, clearly narrow waist, wider hips, long strong legs, toned bare arms, a long dark braid falling from under the helm. Same height. The same ivory linen tunic re-cut for her: shaped to her bust, cinched at the waist by the wide crimson sash with one bronze victory token, a short flared skirt with a side slit; a short crimson cape to the shoulder blades, strapped sandals laced to mid-calf, the bronze Corinthian helm pushed up on the forehead with a short crimson crest, face fully visible. Fierce, elegant and confident, not vulgar. Copper-bronze #A8552E, vivid crimson #C8102E, ivory #E8DCC0, matte, no glow. Keep her feminine cut and the same amount of visible skin. ``

3. Cabeças

3.1 Prompt da cabeça (entrada = o corpo vestido; trocar o trecho entre colchetes pela descrição de 3.2):

`` Head and bare neck only of this character: [DESCRIÇÃO DA CABEÇA]. Same bronze Corinthian helm pushed up on top of the head with a short stiff crimson horsehair crest (always worn), face fully visible, no visor, heroic stylized fantasy face, not photorealistic, front view, plain grey background, neutral light, no shoulders, no clothing, matte, no glow. ``

3.2 Descrições (3 + 3, todas distintas; o elmo fica):

  • M1: man in his forties, short cropped dark hair and a thick trimmed beard, a scar across the left brow, hard calm eyes.
  • M2: young man, clean-shaven, dark curly hair cropped tight at the sides, strong jaw, confident half smile.
  • M3: older man, grey-streaked hair tied back in a short tail, a braided grey beard, deep sun-lines, heavy-lidded eyes.
  • F1: woman, long dark hair in a braided crown held by a crimson ribbon, strong jaw, amber eyes.
  • F2: woman in her thirties, a low ponytail held by a bronze ring, a small scar on the chin, sharp green eyes.
  • F3: young woman, short cropped curls, high cheekbones, brown skin, fierce dark eyes.

4. Mãos

`` Male hand pair, both hands only, relaxed open fingers, palms down, same skin tone as the Gladiator, a plain leather wrist strap at each wrist, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no ring, no weapon, no glow. ` ` Female hand pair, both hands only, strong slender relaxed open fingers, palms down, same skin tone as the female Gladiator, a plain leather wrist strap at each wrist, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no ring, no weapon, no glow. ``

5. Cadeia de níveis (1 ao 150)

Entradas: HOMEM = corpo base masculino em pose T (§1.2); MULHER = corpo base feminino em pose T (§2); LANÇA e ESCUDO = o item do nível anterior (nível 1: os do conceito escolhido), sempre REDESIGNED. Ordem: 1, 6, 11, 15, 21, 26, 31, 37 (HOMEM, MULHER, LANÇA e ESCUDO completos); do 41 ao 150, modelo de ramos por nível: ITEM neutro, HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A, MULHER-B e uma LANÇA e um ESCUDO únicos.

Nível 1 · S1 · Linho e Couro (piloto)

Parte de: corpo base. HOMEM `` Same Gladiator as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. NEW back and grip: a leather arm band and a rope handle. Bigger and heavier-looking than the previous level. Front view, plain light grey background, no hands, no glow. ``

Nível 6 · S1 · Couro Curtido

Parte de: corpo base. HOMEM `` Same Gladiator as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. Unforgettable silhouette: a TALL CRIMSON HORSEHAIR CREST on a bronze Corinthian helm that is ALWAYS on the head, worn pushed up high with the face fully visible (the crest grows taller and wider at every series), a crimson cape at the back (short, never below the waist, until level 40; long from level 41), a sculpted chest (ivory linen and leather at low level, a muscled bronze cuirass later), BARE muscular arms with bronze arm rings, a flared skirt of leather strips (pteruges) and tall bronze greaves: a broad V-shaped hero under a crest. COLOR CONTRAST (must read from far away): warm reddish copper-bronze #A8552E with lighter worn edges, STRONG saturated crimson #C8102E on the crest, cape and sash as the most vivid color of the figure, clean IVORY linen #E8DCC0 as a light third tone (under-tunic, hems, borders), dark leather #3B2418 only for straps; copper, crimson and ivory clearly separated, never a monochrome brown figure. FEMALE CUT: a real heroine's build, never a slimmed-down man: the chest piece is shaped to her bust and toned abs, the belt cinches a clearly narrow waist, the pteruges flare from her wider hips, long athletic legs, a long braid falling from under the helm; fierce and elegant, not vulgar. Copper-bronze #A8552E, vivid crimson #C8102E, ivory #E8DCC0, dark leather #3B2418, matte, no glow. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. Keep her feminine cut. ``

Unforgettable silhouette: a TALL CRIMSON HORSEHAIR CREST on a bronze Corinthian helm that is ALWAYS on the head, worn pushed up high with the face fully visible (the crest grows taller and wider at every series), a crimson cape at the back (short, never below the waist, until level 40; long from level 41), a sculpted chest (ivory linen and leather at low level, a muscled bronze cuirass later), BARE muscular arms with bronze arm rings, a flared skirt of leather strips (pteruges) and tall bronze greaves: a broad V-shaped hero under a crest. Signature detail: champion VICTORY TOKENS, round bronze discs won in the arena, hung from the sash at low level and set as medallions (phalerae) on a chest harness later, more of them at every level, never fewer. COLOR CONTRAST (must read from far away): warm reddish copper-bronze #A8552E with lighter worn edges, STRONG saturated crimson #C8102E on the crest, cape and sash as the most vivid color of the figure, clean IVORY linen #E8DCC0 as a light third tone (under-tunic, hems, borders), dark leather #3B2418 only for straps; copper, crimson and ivory clearly separated, never a monochrome brown figure. Matte, no glow. CRYSTAL TOUCH (subtle): a few small discreet pale-blue crystal inlays with a faint soft inner glow, a quiet accent only, never dominant. ``

6. Arte de apresentação (Gladiador: nível 50 e nível 150)

Duas versões de arte de capa e retrato, uma no nível 50 e outra no nível 150. Entrada: as imagens aprovadas de HOMEM e MULHER do nível indicado, do mesmo ramo (se quiser as duas variantes, repetir com o outro ramo). O nível 50 usa o traje do nível 48, o último vestido ao chegar nos 50; o nível 150 usa o 150-A ou o 150-B. Anexar também a arma aprovada do nível.

Versão nível 50

KEY ART (anexar HOMEM e MULHER juntos) `` Epic key art for the Gladiator class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (male and female) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their engraved bronze spears and round bronze aspis shields in powerful poses, the Pedrabrota quarry pit arena at golden hour, sand floor, tiered stone stands, banners and a mountain pass behind, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO (um por personagem: HOMEM e MULHER, um de cada vez) ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. Three-quarter view, confident look, dramatic rim light, dark charcoal background, painterly game portrait style, high detail, square 1:1, no text. ``

Versão nível 150

KEY ART (anexar HOMEM e MULHER juntos) `` Epic key art for the Gladiator class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (male and female) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their prestige great spears with crystal halos and grand prestige aspis shields with gold filigree in powerful poses, the same quarry arena at night under a vast sky, the stands full of torchlight, a huge ring of cut crystal glinting above the pass, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO (um por personagem: HOMEM e MULHER, um de cada vez) ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. Three-quarter view, confident look, dramatic rim light, dark charcoal background, painterly game portrait style, high detail, square 1:1, no text. ``

7. Prompts FIXOS de arte do item (fundo carvão)

Entrada: a imagem do traje aprovado (para ARMADURA) ou da arma aprovada (para ARMA; vale também para o livro, o escudo e a lança). Os mesmos dois prompts valem para todo nível; nunca editar.

ARMADURA: `` The full torso armor from the input image displayed alone as a game item, including ALL its parts: collar or cape, both pauldrons, chest pieces and straps, belt and the full skirt. No boots, no bracers, no gloves, no arms, no body. Front view, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail. ` ARMA: ` The same weapon from the input image displayed alone as a game item: identical design, materials and colors. Diagonal angle from bottom-left to top-right, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail, no hands, no text. ``

Ragna Tale é um nome de trabalho; pode mudar antes do lançamento.
Jogo em desenvolvimento. Imagens são conceitos de design, não capturas do jogo final. Sem cookies e sem rastreadores.
